Work-wise, I've had to ask dispatch to repeat things. Clients, too. Just details I should have tracked. And, uh... I guess a couple of sales follow-ups slipped through the cracks lately. I just had too many ideas going on at once. \n\nAnd at home... well... my wife and daughter are kind of walking on eggshells. I can tell I'm adding strain there when I'm supposed to be helping. So... yeah. It's affecting things.\n\nDoctor: Have you been more physically active than usual lately?\n\nPatient: Um... I guess so. Yeah. \n\nI just... I've been keeping busy. Finding extra things to do. Like, uh, redoing the route plans, or checking up on the new guys when I'm off the clock. And at home, I'm just... fixing things. \n\nI just get kind of antsy if I have to sit still for too long. So I keep moving.\n\nDoctor: Have you been acting on impulses or doing things without thinking them through lately?\n\nPatient: I don't think so. I mean... I'm just doing what needs to get done. I haven't done anything crazy. Just keeping busy, like I said.\n\nDoctor: How much distress or anxiety are these difficulties with focusing and getting sidetracked causing you?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I wouldn't really say it's causing me anxiety. \n\nIt's just... a little frustrating sometimes, I guess. Having to double-check things or ask people to repeat themselves. But I'm not really stressed out about it. It just is what it is.\n\nDoctor: Are you also fidgety or restless when you're at home or in other situations where you're not working?\n\nPatient: Yeah... like I said before. I just find extra jobs for myself around the house. Fixing things up, mostly. I just get kind of antsy if I sit around too long, so... I stay busy.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself getting sidetracked or having trouble focusing when you're not working, like when you're relaxing or doing leisure activities?\n\nPatient: Not really. I don't really do much relaxing, honestly. If I'm not working, I'm usually just finding something else to do. I don't really sit around enough to get sidetracked from it.\n\nDoctor: How old were you when you first started noticing these difficulties with focusing and getting sidetracked?\n\nPatient: I don't know... maybe about seven months ago? That's when my daughter moved back in with me. I just... I started trying to keep everything steady around the house, and I guess that's when I started noticing it more.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself talking more than others in social situations?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I'm... not much of a talker. I mostly just keep to myself.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Just keeping busy, like I said.\n\nDoctor: How much distress or anxiety are these difficulties with focusing and getting sidetracked causing you?\n\nPatient: I don't know. I wouldn't really say it's causing me anxiety. \n\nIt's just... a little frustrating sometimes, I guess. Having to double-check things or ask people to repeat themselves. But I'm not really stressed out about it. It just is what it is.\n\nDoctor: Are you also fidgety or restless when you're at home or in other situations where you're not working?\n\nPatient: Yeah... like I said before. I stay busy.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself getting sidetracked or having trouble focusing when you're not working, like when you're relaxing or doing leisure activities?\n\nPatient: Not really. I don't really do much relaxing, honestly. If I'm not working, I'm usually just finding something else to do. I don't really sit around enough to get sidetracked from it.\n\nDoctor: How old were you when you first started noticing these difficulties with focusing and getting sidetracked?\n\nPatient: I don't know... maybe about seven months ago? That's when my daughter moved back in with me. I just... I started trying to keep everything steady around the house, and I guess that's when I started noticing it more.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find yourself talking more than others in social situations?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I'm... not much of a talker. I mostly just keep to myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you always had some trouble with focusing and getting sidetracked, even when you were younger?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. Like I said, it's mostly just been the last few months. I don't remember it being an issue before that.\n\nDoctor: Are you experiencing these difficulties with focusing and getting sidetracked at any time of the day, or are they mainly happening at specific times?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure... I guess it just happens whenever.
